## Phase 4 / Task 13 notes (Sessions screen, re-authentication prompt, logout warning in the app)
- Step done:
  - `crates/app/src/sessions.rs`: pure `SessionsModel` (devices, rename field, sign-out confirmation, `pending` device IDs, `ReauthPrompt`, account-page `Approval`, notice, logout warning), `SessionsMessage`, pure helpers `logout_needs_warning`, `device_title`, `device_id_line`, `trust_label`, `can_verify`, `last_active_text` (uses `dates.rs` labels), `dedup_ids`, and the `App` handlers.
  - `crates/app/src/screens/sessions.rs`: the page (Refresh, Close, rows, "Sign out of all other sessions"), the password card and the logout-warning card, layered over the main view; `main_view` status bar gains **Sessions**.
  - `state.rs`: `Message::Sessions`, `App.sessions`; routes `Devices`, `ActionFailed { Devices | SignOutDevices }` (a `Devices` failure never ends a sign-out; `SignOutDevices` does, since d00f1fb), `ReauthRequired { DeleteDevices }` and `OpenAccountManagement` (to the security screens while a reset runs, otherwise to Sessions); `LogoutRequested` asks first when `logout_needs_warning`; `drop_secrets` clears the sessions password prompt; `back_to_login` resets the model.
- Ledger notes honoured:
  - Task 8 (no re-emit after renames and deletions by other clients): opening the screen and **Refresh** send `ListDevices`. An unseen device may list as `NoKeys` until the sync keys query; the badge says "No encryption keys" and offers no Verify (Refresh later).
  - Task 8 (OAuth): several devices open the DevicesList page; the app just opens the URL it is given (after the checks below).
  - Task 8 (names attacker-controlled): every name goes through `text_safety` (`name_or_placeholder`, 64 chars); a blank name shows the device ID; the ID is always shown too (elided in the middle, never cut from the end); the rename field starts with the sanitized name and caps input at 64 characters.
  - Task 8 (`uia_password` plain String): the app keeps the typed password only in the prompt's `Password` (zeroized); on Continue it moves into the command and the field is left empty; `pending` holds device IDs only; Cancel, close, logout and a finished sign-out drop the prompt.
  - Task 8 (no dedup or cap in `sign_out_devices`): the app dedups (`dedup_ids`), drops this device and unknown IDs, and keeps at most 500 devices from the list.
  - Task 4/5 (`ReauthRequired(DeleteDevices)` ignored): now handled only while a sign-out of the user's is pending; a wrong password (`ReauthFailed`) keeps the prompt, empties the field and shows "Wrong password"; any other failure ends the sign-out with a one-line notice.
  - Task 10 (OAuth URLs): the account-page URL goes through `check_browser_url`, must also pass `check_link_url` as `https`, and shows a bar naming the host; the browser starts only inside a `Task` after **Open**. A refused address gives a notice and nothing opens.
  - Task 11: **Verify** (unverified other devices only) sends `RequestDeviceVerification`; the dialog opens from the core's events as for any request. No user or device ID is cut from the end.
- Decisions to confirm:
  - `logout_needs_warning(status, other_devices: Option<usize>)`: warns when recovery is `Disabled` or `Incomplete` (not `Enabled`) and there is no other session; an unknown list (Sessions never opened) warns too, to be safe. A recovery state still `Unknown` does not warn. The list is the last one the core sent, so it can be stale.
  - The password prompt is the Sessions screen's own card (the security screens' prompt stays for reset and bootstrap).
  - After an OAuth sign-out the app does not know the outcome: the bar tells the user to press Refresh after approving.
- TDD: pure helpers and the `update` tests were seen failing on assertions against stubs. Two existing logout tests (Task 10) now set recovery `Enabled` first, because logout with recovery off warns. No view test beyond a smoke test that builds every Sessions state.
- APIs confirmed in `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/`: iced_widget-0.14.2 `helpers.rs:561` (`stack(children)`), `helpers.rs:1444` (`text_input`), `text_input.rs:157,163,184,201` (`id`, `secure`, `on_input_maybe`, `on_submit_maybe`); the rest is already used elsewhere in the crate.
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (app lib 555), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`: pass for the first build (71f9702). Correction: the later round d00f1fb did change core code (`ActionKind::SignOutDevices` and its failure paths), so the ignored suites were not "unneeded": the controller ran the ignored `e2ee_devices` suite (5 tests, two assertions updated) against the local homeserver after d00f1fb, green in about 41 s; the full ignored suite ran in Task 14 (see "Phase 4 wrap-up").
- Review fix (Low, security): a sign-out and an identity reset no longer overlap. While a reset runs (`Busy::Resetting`), `SignOutAsk`, `SignOutAllAsk` and `SignOutConfirmed` send nothing and show a one-line notice (confirmation dropped); while a sign-out is pending, `ResetRequested` and `ResetConfirmed` send nothing and show a one-line notice (`refuse_sign_out_during_reset` in `sessions.rs`, `refuse_reset_during_sign_out` in `security.rs`). So an OAuth account page URL can only belong to the flow that is running. Two `update` tests (mpsc sink) were seen failing on assertions first; the visible notices are plain text, no extra human check.
- Review fix (Medium, security): closing the Sessions screen no longer forgets a sign-out the core is still running. `Close` drops the password prompt, confirmation and approval bar but keeps `pending` until the core answers (`Devices` without the targets, `ActionFailed { SignOutDevices }` (first built as `ActionFailed { Devices }`, replaced by `ActionKind::SignOutDevices` in d00f1fb), `ReauthRequired`, `OpenAccountManagement`, logout), so `ResetRequested`/`ResetConfirmed` stay refused. `OpenAccountManagement` goes to the Sessions approval bar whenever a sign-out is pending (even if `Busy::Resetting`), never to the reset's browser path; the bar waits for the user's click and stays until the screen is opened. A `ReauthRequired` with the screen closed ends the sign-out quietly (no prompt appears on its own; the user starts it again). Three `update` tests (mpsc sink) were seen failing on assertions first.
- Review fix (Low, superseded by the next note): a sign-out used to end on a list of core error values copied from private constants.
- Review fix (Low): `submit_reauth` for a `ResetIdentity` prompt checks `refuse_reset_during_sign_out` first (sends nothing, notice); `refuse_sign_out_during_reset` also treats an open `ResetIdentity` password prompt as a reset in progress. Four `update` tests (mpsc sink) were seen failing on assertions first.
- Review fix (human-approved additive core change): `ActionKind::SignOutDevices`. Every failure of the `SignOutDevices` command path (not logged in, precheck, delete flow, the post-delete device reload, the OAuth account-page message) uses it; error values are unchanged. The app ends a pending sign-out on any `ActionFailed { SignOutDevices, .. }` (a `ReauthFailed` with the password prompt open still keeps the prompt) and shows a one-line notice; `ActionFailed { Devices, .. }` (list, rename, trust watch) never ends it. The copied text constants and the error-value matching are gone, which closes the gap where a failed device reload after a successful delete left `pending` set. Reopening Sessions or Refresh sends `ListDevices`, and a `Devices` answer without the targets still ends the sign-out. Core: unit test of the kind for a sign-out that cannot start; the ignored `e2ee_devices.rs` assertions (wrong password, current device) now expect the new kind. Tests in `state.rs` written first and seen failing on assertions.
- Open issues: the Sessions row has no spinner while a sign-out waits (text only).

## Phase 4 / Task 15 notes (change the recovery key from the Sessions page)
- Added by the human on 2026-10-07 at the checkpoint (Q5 "Change key" had no screen); plan written as Task 15 in `docs/plans/phase4-e2ee.md` and approved the same day. App only: no core, API, dependency or bridge change. The core's `ChangeRecoveryKey` (Task 5, `Recovery::reset_key()`, matrix-sdk 0.19.1 `encryption/recovery/mod.rs:359`) and its integration test (new key differs, opens a fresh store, old key fails) are reused as they are.
- `crates/app/src/sessions.rs`: `RecoveryAction` (`ChangeKey`, `SetUp`, `EnterKey`, with `label` and `message`) and the pure `recovery_line(RecoveryStatus)`: Enabled "Recovery is on." + **Change recovery key**; Disabled "Recovery is off." + **Set up recovery**; Incomplete "This device isn't connected to your recovery." + **Enter recovery key**; Unknown: no line. `screens/sessions.rs` draws it as a bordered row above the device list (shown while the list loads too); the button is off while any security `Busy` is set.
- `crates/app/src/security.rs`: `Overlay::ChangeKeyConfirm`, `Busy::ChangingKey`, `SecurityMessage::{ChangeKeyRequested, ChangeKeyConfirmed, ChangeKeyCancelled}`, `RecoverySetup.replaces_old` and `key_title` ("Your new recovery key" after a change, "Your recovery key" after set-up). Requested opens the card only when recovery is `Enabled`, nothing is busy and no key screen is open; Confirmed sends one `ChangeRecoveryKey` and keeps the card with "Changing your recovery key…"; Cancel is ignored once sent. `RecoveryKeyReady` during `ChangingKey` closes the card and opens the show-once screen (Done gate, Copy, 60 s clipboard clear and the drop on Done, logout and close unchanged). `ActionFailed { Recovery }` during `ChangingKey` clears busy, closes the card and puts the core's fixed message on the Sessions page notice (the main view's notice bar is under the page).
- `screens/security.rs`: `CHANGE_KEY_WARNING` (the plan's draft wording) and `CHANGE_KEY_WAIT`; the card uses the existing `wide` and `secondary` buttons.
- TDD: the 7 `state.rs` tests and the `sessions.rs` test were written first and failed to compile (missing variants, `key_title`, `recovery_line`), then passed: asks first and Cancel sends nothing; one command and nothing while busy (also **Set up recovery**); refused unless recovery is on (Disabled, Incomplete, Unknown); new key screen titled as new, Done needs the box, back on the Sessions page; set-up keeps the first title; failure on the Sessions page; the card, the wait and the new key screen fill a 1000 x 700 window, and the new key screen's `Debug` holds none of the sweep sentinels.
- No integration test (as planned): the E2EE reset helpers live in `crates/core/tests/common`, which app tests cannot reach.
- APIs: no new matrix-sdk or iced API; the views use only widgets and methods already used in the same files (`button::on_press_maybe`, `container::bordered_box`, `row`, `text`, `space`).
- Checks: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (935 passed, 0 failed; app lib 577), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` all pass. No ignored run: no core or bridge code changed.
- New human items (checkpoint list and checklist page): the Recovery line in each state and both themes; the warning wording and the "Your new recovery key" screen; a changed key restores in Element Web (as bob) and the old one is refused.

## Phase 4 security review (Task 14, plan Q15, Q16 (d))
Written by the agent from the code and the tests; the human accepts or rejects the residuals (last item of the checkpoint).

### Where each secret lives
| Secret | Where | Protection |
|---|---|---|
| Access token, refresh token (if any), the SQLite store passphrase | The OS keychain, one `SessionRecord` JSON entry per account (service `io.github.ga_lep.Tessera`); `SessionRecord::to_bytes` is the only serializer | OS keychain; `Debug` of the record prints `<redacted>`; in the core only `store_passphrase` is `Zeroizing`, and the `to_bytes` output; the tokens are plain `String`s inside the SDK types (`SessionAuth::Matrix(MatrixSession)`, `SessionAuth::OAuth { user: UserSession }`), `SessionRecord` and `SessionAuth` are `Clone`, and the live SDK `Client` keeps its own copies |
| Room keys, Olm/Megolm sessions, cross-signing private keys, backup key, message events | The SDK's SQLite store under `<data>/sessions/<session id>/` (main, `-wal`, `-shm`) | Values encrypted with the store cipher derived from the keychain passphrase; keys of the tables are hashed |
| User ID of the last account | `<data>/last_account` | Plain text on purpose (it is not a secret); the data root holds only this file and `sessions/` (unit test in `paths.rs`) |
| Recovery key (new or typed), recovery passphrase | Never stored by Tessera or the SDK. The recovery key encrypts secret storage (4S: the backup key and the cross-signing keys), which lives on the server; the key itself is not kept. The core hands a new key to the app once (`RecoveryKeyReady`) | App: `RecoveryKey` wrapper (`Zeroizing`, redacted `Debug`); the show-once screen drops it on **Done**, logout and close; a copy on the clipboard is cleared after 60 s, on logout and on close if it still holds the key |
| Account password (login, UIA for reset, bootstrap and sign-out of devices) | Never stored. Held in the typed field only until submit | `Password` wrapper (`Zeroizing`, redacted `Debug`); moved into the command on submit; every prompt is dropped on Cancel, close, logout |
| QR code (one-time verification secret) | On screen only while the QR phases last | The core prints `QrImage` as its size only; the app builds the image on `ShowQr` and drops it on every other phase |
| SAS emoji and numbers | On screen during the comparison | Not secret after the flow; the app's `Debug` of the dialog prints the phase name only |

### Evidence
- **Log capture (Q16 a):** `crates/core/tests/log_hygiene.rs` (ignored, own binary). One flow (alice and carol after `reset_e2ee_account`: password login, sync, encrypted DM with sentinel bodies both ways, identity reset, recovery enable, second device that recovers with the key, SAS between the two alice devices, device rename, logout) under a global `tracing_subscriber` at filter `info` and, in a second buffer, `DEFAULT_FILTER`. 12 sentinel values (3 access tokens, 3 store passphrases, the passwords, the recovery key, the message bodies) were searched in both buffers: none found. A self-check (a logged sentinel and a scratch-file sentinel must be found, an unrelated one not) was seen red.
- **Data at rest (Q16 b):** the same test, before logout, reads every file under the data root (39 files, `-wal` and `-shm` included) and searches the same sentinels: none found. The scan covers the literal values the test knows, not derived forms (base64, URL-encoded).
- **Keychain-only audit (Q16 c):** unit tests in `paths.rs` (the data root holds only `last_account` and `sessions/<id>`) and `session.rs` (`SessionRecord` `Debug` and error strings carry no sentinel; `Serialize` and `serde_json::to_*` appear in no other source file, a tripwire only). These pinned existing behaviour (not seen red).
- **App redaction sweep (Task 14):** `state.rs` test `the_redaction_sweep_finds_no_secret_in_any_state_or_message` builds an `App` in the show-once key screen, a restore input, the reset password prompt, the Sessions password prompt, a verification in `Emojis` and one in `ShowQr`, and the Sessions screen with a rename in progress, with sentinel values, and finds none in `format!("{app:?}")`; the QR modules are not printed. It also checks the `Debug` of `secret_messages()`: `RecoveryKeyReady`, `RestoreInput`, `ClipboardRead`, `ClipboardFlushed`, both `ReauthInput`, the `ShowQr` event, the login `PasswordChanged`, the `OAuthUrl` and `OpenAccountManagement` events (sentinel in the URL `state`), the composer `Paste` and `Insert` actions, `PickerQuery`, `PickerPicked` and `ReactionToggled`; the states cover the login form, the waiting-for-browser screen, and the composer and picker with typed text. Not covered: messages that carry only IDs, flags or an index. The browser-link state check is weak: `App`'s `Debug` does not print `browser_link`, so a planted leak in `BrowserUrl`'s `Debug` was not caught (the message-level `OAuthUrl` check uses `AuthUrl`'s redaction). It passed at once: it pins existing behaviour (the manual `Debug` impls from Tasks 10, 11, 13).
- **App logging tripwire (Task 14):** `crates/app/tests/no_secret_logging.rs` scans the non-test source of `crates/app/src` for `println!`, `print!`, `eprintln!`, `eprint!`, `dbg!`, `write!`, `writeln!`, `trace!`, `debug!`, `info!`, `warn!`, `error!`, `tracing::event!`, `log::`, `panic!`, `assert!`, `assert_eq!`, `assert_ne!`, their `debug_` forms, `unreachable!`, `todo!`, `unimplemented!` and `.expect(`, and fails when their arguments name a secret-bearing type, field or model (list in the file). It passed at once against the existing code (the app has no `tracing` dependency; the only prints are command-line errors in `main.rs`). To show it bites, a planted `eprintln!("{}", setup.key.expose())` in `security.rs` made it fail ("eprintln! with `.expose(`"), and was removed. A second test feeds the scanner planted violations for every macro family and checks that comments and `#[cfg(test)]` items are ignored.
- **Not asserted:** SDK `debug`/`trace` logging (the SDK logs request details at `debug` by design; documented in `logging.rs` as local debugging only), and the pixels and texts iced renders.

### Residuals (to accept or reject)
- **Tokens in memory:** the access and refresh tokens are plain `String`s in memory (SDK types, live `Client`); only the store passphrase and `to_bytes` output are `Zeroizing` in the core, and `SessionRecord`/`SessionAuth` are `Clone`.
- **`RUST_LOG`:** it replaces the default filter of the stderr subscriber installed by `crates/app/src/main.rs` (`logging::init`, core `logging.rs` `build_filter`), so a user can enable SDK `debug`/`trace` request logging; not covered by `log_hygiene`.
- **Scanner limits (`no_secret_logging.rs`):** it is a tripwire for ordinary mistakes, not a guarantee. It misses `format!` into a local that is printed later, direct writes such as `io::stderr().write_all(..)`, and a secret copied into an innocently named local; code under `#[cfg(test)]` is skipped on purpose. It matches names as text, so deliberate bypasses pass: a space before `!`, raw identifiers (`r#password`), whitespace around `.` or `(`, type aliases and qualified calls (`K::expose`, `Option::expect(x, msg)`), `.security` on receivers other than `self`, and unlisted macros (`format_args!`, `write_fmt`, a bare `event!`/`log!`/`span!` after `use`, `#[tracing::instrument]`). Comments, strings, raw strings and character literals are skipped by one shared lexer step.
- **iced buffers (Q15):** `text_input` keeps plain copies of typed text (the password, the recovery key) in its own and the renderer's text and paragraph buffers; they are not zeroized. The same holds for the Phase 3 editor buffer. `Password` and `RecoveryKey` zeroize only the app's own copies. The SDK also keeps plain `String` copies of a typed key during `recover()`, and ruma's `uiaa::Password` holds the password in a plain `String` while a UIA request is built.
- **Clipboard:** after **Copy** the key leaves `Zeroizing` (iced's clipboard takes and returns `String`); OS clipboard managers and their history can keep it after the 60 s clear, which only touches the live clipboard (checkpoint item; no clipboard-manager hint exists in iced or arboard).
- **On-screen secrets:** the recovery key on the show-once screen and the QR code during a flow are visible to anyone who can see the screen; the QR RGBA buffer is not wiped and iced's renderer may cache the image after the handle is dropped. The SAS emoji are printed by the derived `Debug` of the core's `VerificationPhase::Emojis` (they are not logged anywhere, and only matter during the comparison).
- **At rest:** the user ID in `last_account` is plain text; the SQLite table layout, row counts and sizes are visible (matrix-sdk-sqlite encrypts values and hashes keys, it is not whole-file encryption); the store passphrase is as safe as the OS keychain.
- **`RecoveryKey`'s `PartialEq`** is not constant-time (used in tests and for the clipboard comparison only); `QrImage` is not zeroized.
- **Server and other users:** another user's device names and display names are attacker-controlled; the app sanitizes bidi, control and zero-width characters, caps lengths, elides IDs in the middle (never from the end), and shows the full peer ID in DMs and verification requests. Lax shields (`get_shield(false)`) as Element X.
- **Open Phase 3 security items carried over:** the editor buffer is not zeroized (Q17 of Phase 3); name ambiguity (reply previews, history actors and typing names carry no core ambiguity flag, so a duplicate display name can impersonate); **Copy** writes the raw plain body to the clipboard (hidden control and bidi characters, pastejacking); the residual body-styling spoof and mention label spoofing (Phase 3 open issues).

## Phase 4 open issues (summary)
Every Low or Medium finding from the task reviews (`docs/plans/phase4-e2ee.progress.md`), by status. "Fixed" means a later round closed it with the commit named; "open" items stay for the human or a later phase.

### Fixed by later rounds
- **Task 4 first-bootstrap Medium (M1) and password-less reset Medium (M2):** fixed in 6291447 (raw server keys query, `bootstrap_plan`, password-less reset refused on UIA sessions). The identity-permit High and the password-retry Medium: d172d3f. The reset/sync race that made `e2ee_identity` time out: 2d3b383, then 23fbcf6 (`SyncPause`).
- **Task 5 prechecks and reset races:** 23fbcf6 and ae10b8b. Drop-spawned restore: pause made panic-safe (ae10b8b); race with logout still open (see below).
- **Task 6 `WatchUserTrust` keys-query race:** the tests look the peer up first; the app does it on opening a DM (Task 12). Task 8 refined the watcher.
- **Task 9 send stall after a reset:** 313f356 (Security event held until sync and send queue are restored); the SDK-timeline-clear report was retracted (test bug, 83ef109).
- **Task 10 copied key left on the clipboard on logout or close:** a2c94ae and 4e3abc7 (flush on close, restore field emptied).
- **Task 11 peer user ID cut from the end (Medium):** fixed in 3cc2423 (elided in the middle, full ID on its own line); a request behind a finished card is queued.
- **Task 12 DM Verified badge next to an attacker-settable room name, and the app guessing the DM peer from timeline senders (two Mediums):** fixed in 8a38fd5 (`RoomSummary.dm_peer` from the core, full peer ID in the header, `VerifyPeerRequested` checked against it).
- **Task 13 sign-out and identity reset overlap (Medium, and Lows):** 87b7cc3, aeead79, ae3db3f, d00f1fb (`ActionKind::SignOutDevices`; a sign-out ends only on its own failures).
- **Task 13 stale PROGRESS.md notes** (ignored suites "not run", `ActionFailed { Devices }`): corrected in acb0478.

### Also fixed in the app
- **Task 6 and Task 8 attacker-controlled device and peer names (Info/Low):** the app sanitizes bidi, control and zero-width characters (Tasks 11 and 13).
- **Task 11 a new request replacing a finished card under the mouse (Low):** queued behind it (3cc2423).

### Open: Low or Medium findings
Core:
- **Cross-phase (Phase 1 limitation, re-raised in the Task 14 review):** access and refresh tokens are plain `String`s in SDK types and the live `Client`; `SessionRecord`/`SessionAuth` are `Clone`; only `store_passphrase` and the `to_bytes` output are `Zeroizing` (see Residuals).
- **Task 1:** `seed_recovery` passphrase and key not zeroized end to end (test-only); `e2ee_serial` serializes per binary only (a parallel runner such as nextest could overlap resets); a panicking test leaves its device until the next reset; the `fetch_raw_event_type` check in the room smoke test is weak.
- **Task 2:** `RecoveryKey` derived `PartialEq` is not constant-time; `QrImage` grid not zeroized; the CLI `recovery_key_event_prints_no_key` test is weak (does not capture output); non-room actions send `ActionFailed` with an empty `RoomId` (the app routes by action).
- **Task 3:** `RoomSummary.is_encrypted` is false while the SDK state is Unknown (the app draws a lock for `true` only); a room that just turned encrypted may get its lock only on its next list update.
- **Task 4:** a sync-driven keys query during the OAuth wait can make a cancelled reset report success (Task 5's 23fbcf6 made `reset_end` require complete private keys, which may have narrowed this; not re-verified; otherwise compare against the private identity or require a complete cross-signing status); a wrong password, an Unsupported flow or an OAuth cancel still leaves the backup deleted (the app says so); a millisecond-wide check-then-upload window in the bootstrap retry; `keys_gate` stalls `Security` events for up to 2 minutes during the OAuth approval; a sync-driven keys query racing `keys_gate` is not covered by it; `uiaa::Password` is not zeroized; the first-upload-with-UIA path is covered by a fake only; `has_devices_to_verify_against` counts the own device right after a reset.
- **Task 5:** a Drop-spawned restore after a panic can race logout; bootstrap or reset before `start_sync` runs unpaused; an in-flight send's key query is not covered by disabling the queue; an abort during `pause()` leaves sync stopped (benign today); `wait_for_e2ee_initialization_tasks` has no timeout; prechecks read the cached `recovery.state()`; `ChangeRecoveryKey` drops other clients' secrets stored under the old key; sync shows `Idle` and no messages arrive during a reset (up to about 2 minutes with OAuth).
- **Task 6:** a request from a never-seen device can still be lost; a peer's `UserTrust::Verified` can lag about 30 s; every request event (also in-room ones not for us) spawns an uncapped lookup of about 3 s (skip `content.to != me`, add a semaphore); a stranger's request can hold the single slot until declined; test asserts can print message bodies (`e2ee_verification.rs:365`) and device names on failure; `watch_user_trust` aborts the old watcher without awaiting; the self-verification test does not assert core 2's `Unverified` after login, and the mismatch test does not check core 1's trust unchanged.
- **Task 7:** the `map_qr_state` test lacks the `Cancelled` arm; an incoming request with no QR code emits nothing (stays `Ready`) where the plan says `Waiting`; a `generate_qr_code()` error sends `ActionFailed` and then falls back to SAS (spurious notice).
- **Task 8:** an unseen device lists as `NoKeys` until the sync keys query; renames and deletions by other clients are not re-emitted (the app re-asks on Refresh); `watch_devices` keeps the old list on later read errors; `uia_password` copies the password into a plain `String`; `sign_out_devices` does not dedup or cap the ID list (the app does); on an OAuth account several selected devices open the devices-list page instead of a per-device URL (deviation to confirm at the checkpoint); a stale `UserTrust` from an aborted watcher can follow the new one (the app filters by user ID).
- **Task 9:** `settled()` waits the full 10 s holding the sync gate and `paused` when offline or on a sync error, and can return at once on a stale `Running` (the timeout is silent); a status can still go out mid-pause by a narrow gap (promptness only; `keys_gate` protects); no mock test pins "Security held until restore"; `log_hygiene` keeps unzeroized `serde_json::Value` copies (test only).
App:
- **Task 10:** the copied key leaves `Zeroizing` (documented residual above); the approval URL doc comment says "discovered https endpoint" but only the scheme is checked, host not pinned (fix the comment or compare hosts via `login::host_of`); the password prompt for a cross-signing setup shows the reset wait wording; the bridge test's identity-screen assertion is conditional; "backup already exists" is matched by a substring of the core's `Internal` text (a dedicated `CoreError` variant would be sturdier, a core API change); the Change-key screen (Q5) is not built.
- **Task 11:** QR pixels may linger in iced's renderer cache; a request during a running flow is dropped silently and a third request overwrites the queued one; a late failure shows "Could not start the verification"; a failed confirm in a running verification flow is silent (it only re-enables the buttons); the waiting cards show a text line (iced 0.14 has no spinner widget); the queue test does not assert `asked_other_device`, the queued-overwrite deserves a comment.
- **Task 12:** `dm_peer` comes from the account's own `m.direct`, not from membership (an invited third member does not remove the Verified badge); a changed `m.direct` target may show only on the room's next list update; U+1F512 font coverage is unchecked; the header trust map keeps the Phase 3 measured height (plan wording loose).
- **Other Info and suggestions:** Task 4 comment on `server_identity_seen` never clearing; Task 7 doc note that `StartSas` outside a QR flow is `NotAllowed` on purpose; Task 9 a send right after login is not tested separately; Task 10 the `on_status` half of the restore-field clearing has no test; Task 13 the approval bar host is ASCII (non-ASCII refused).
- **Task 13:** the logout warning is skipped while recovery is `Unknown` or the list is stale, and any other listed session (even unverified or `NoKeys`) suppresses it (consider `Unknown` warns, count `Verified` only); `BootstrapCrossSigning` is not fenced against a pending sign-out (harmless today, tag `OpenAccountManagement` with its action); a stale reset URL after a cancelled reset can still be shown for a sign-out; `ReauthRequired` after Close ends a sign-out quietly; after an OAuth sign-out the user must press Refresh; no spinner while a sign-out waits; raw device IDs are sanitized with no length cap before `elide_middle`; `Internal` error text is shown in notices (fixed strings today).

### Scope cuts (Q17), the Synapse rule, the Q3 decision
- **Out of Phase 4 (Q17):** scanning QR codes (no camera) and QR login (MSC4108); identity-change acknowledgement and pinning UI (badge only); turning recovery or backup off (`disable()`); key export/import files; dehydrated devices; history sharing on invite; "send only to verified devices" settings; encrypted state events; the cross-process store lock; creating encrypted rooms from the UI (Phase 5); i18n.
- **Synapse first-upload rule (Task 4):** Synapse v1.151.0 (`keys.py`, `SigningKeyUploadServlet.on_POST`) allows the first upload of cross-signing keys without UIA (MSC3967) and demands UIA for any replacement, so a restored pre-Phase-4 session bootstraps with no password and a reset always needs one. The local fixed accounts cannot produce a never-had-an-identity account, so the first-time path is covered by CI's fresh volume only; a homeserver that demands UIA for a first upload (matrix.org may) is on the checkpoint.
- **Q3 iced widget decision:** matrix-sdk `qrcode` is enabled, iced `qr_code` is NOT enabled; the code is drawn with the existing `image` widget from the SDK's own grid (6 px per module, nearest filter, 288 px, quiet zone). Cargo.lock gained `matrix-sdk-qrcode` 0.19.1 and `qrcode` 0.14.1 only.
- **Per-test reset cost and test durations:** each E2EE test resets alice and carol (about 3 to 6 s per test); `e2ee_recovery` 7 tests take 78 to 84 s, `e2ee_identity` 5 to 7 tests about 45 s, `e2ee_devices` 5 tests about 41 s, `log_hygiene` about 19 s, the app bridge suite about 5 s. Full ignored runs (about 7.5 and 7 minutes) are in "Phase 4 wrap-up".
- **Test-run notes:** the Task 6 peer lookup and the Task 9 post-reset send were the flakes found and fixed in Phase 4. The Task 14 repeat-run failures are the room backlog described in "Phase 4 wrap-up" (not a core bug); run `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh` before an ignored run when the fixed accounts have accumulated rooms.

## Phase 4 wrap-up (Task 14)
- Agent work for Phase 4 is done (Tasks 1 to 14); the human checkpoint below stays open and nothing on it is ticked by the agent (rule 9: rendering, real accounts, Element X, OS clipboard managers, keychains).
- Full ignored suite (`cargo test -p tessera-core -p tessera-app --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring`, as CI) against the local Synapse, 75 tests, all passed in both runs:
  - run 1, right after `scripts/create-test-users.sh`: 457 s (7 min 37 s, wall clock including an incremental build);
  - run 2, after `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh` only (rooms; no `create-test-users.sh`, so devices and identities left by run 1 carry over): 429 s (7 min 9 s). Both are under the 15-minute limit.
- **Decision (human), repeat-run recipe:** the repeat run is `scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh` (rooms only), then the full ignored suite WITHOUT `create-test-users.sh`. Residual: the repeat does not start from fresh accounts; it reuses the devices and identities of the previous run (the E2EE tests reset their baseline themselves, Q2).
- **Failed repeat attempts and root cause (found by a debugger):**
  - run 2 (earlier attempt): 1 failure in `timeline_actions`; run 3: 7 failures in `timeline_actions`, 2 in `timeline_items` and 1 in `timeline_live` (all `apply_until` 30 s timeouts). The earlier attempt stopped BLOCKED (rule 8) on the repeat.
  - Evidence: joined rooms accumulate on the fixed test accounts, about 50 per full run (alice had 151 and carol 97 before the failed repeat). After the first sync the SDK event cache processes every joined room serially (`handle_joined_room_update`, about 0.1 to 0.13 s each in a debug build), and a new test room's events wait behind that backlog. Measured time to receive in `markdown_round_trip` alone: about 0.37 s with 1 room, 3.2 s with 25, 6.5 s with 50, 12.7 s with 100; the 30 s `apply_until` limit is exceeded at about 150 rooms with parallel tests.
  - Conclusion: not a test race and not a core bug. The pinned SDK has no "event cache caught up" signal, so the cure is pruning rooms (`scripts/prune-test-rooms.sh`). Before this repeat, the prune left alice 49, bob 3 and carol 30 rooms to leave.
- Checks for the commit: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace`, `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`.

- ~~Reset identity (Task 4 core): with an OAuth account (matrix.org) the approval page opens from `OpenAccountManagement`, approving finishes the reset, and cancelling it leaves the old identity; with the local Synapse and a password the reset needs the password and a wrong one asks again (core tests cover only the password path and the pure OAuth branch); cancelling reports `Cancelled` (not success) and no approval URL is sent after an early cancel; a wrong password still deletes the backup (SDK order), so check the app asks for the password first.~~ **Not checked, risk accepted (human decision, 2026-10-07):** needs a real matrix.org (OAuth) account, which was not used.
- ~~Cross-signing on a restored session from before Phase 4 (no identity yet) on a homeserver that demands UIA for a first upload (the local Synapse v1.151.0 does not; matrix.org may): the password prompt appears and the identity is created.~~ **Not checked, risk accepted (human decision, 2026-10-07):** needs a real matrix.org (OAuth) account, which was not used.
- ~~Recovery set-up progress (Task 5 core): with an account that has real history, the progress events advance to the end and the key arrives once; `EnableRecovery` on a device that is not connected to an existing backup shows the "enter your recovery key" path; changing the key on a restored device gives a new key and the old one stops working in Element.~~ **Not checked, risk accepted (human decision, 2026-10-07):** needs a real matrix.org (OAuth) account, which was not used.
- [x] Reset right after login and reset on an account with recovery on: the identity ends `Complete` on this device (fixed by pausing the sync during bootstrap/reset, see "Reset race fix"; automated and passing). Human: with the real window, a reset shows a short `Idle` sync state and no new messages while it runs; for an OAuth reset that is up to the 2 minutes of the approval wait. Confirm this is acceptable. Human-confirmed 2026-10-07.
- [x] Verification timing (Task 6 core): after verifying another user, their badge may take up to 30 s to turn verified (SDK uploads the signature with the next sync request); a verification request from a device never seen before may be ignored. Human-confirmed 2026-10-07.

- [x] Security screens (Task 10), both themes: the recovery key is legible in the monospace box and copyable by hand and with Copy; the clipboard is cleared 60 s after Copy only if it still holds the key (paste something else first to check it is left alone); Done stays disabled until the box is ticked. Human-confirmed 2026-10-07.
- [x] Security screens (Task 10): the reset warning, the "Resetting... syncing is paused, up to two minutes" text and the "your key backup was deleted by the reset attempt" line after a wrong password, a cancelled approval or a failed reset read well and are true; restore with a wrong key shows its message and keeps the field. Human-confirmed 2026-10-07.
- [x] Security screens (Task 10): opening the banner or any security screen keeps the timeline scroll and the composer focus; typing in the restore or password field never types into the composer behind it; the screens look right at a small window size. Human-confirmed 2026-10-07.

- [x] Skip for now (Task 10): it leaves the unverified banner; its wording reads well; the banner stays after a restart (a restored session shows the banner, not the screen); it has no dismiss button; its Verify button reopens the Confirm-identity screen. Human-confirmed 2026-10-07.
- [x] Copy then log out or close the window within 60 s (Task 10): the recovery key is no longer on the clipboard afterwards (cleared at once if it still held the key; something else you copied meanwhile is left alone). Human-confirmed 2026-10-07 on Linux only (Wayland and X11); Windows and macOS are the open item at the end.

Action tags unchanged (SHA pinning left to the human). actionlint and shellcheck clean.
- Pipeline fix: `check-app-deps.sh` now assigns the `tail`/`awk` output as a plain statement (set -e applies) and runs `grep` separately, so only a match exits 1, no match exits 0, and any other failure fails closed (before: a failing fake `awk` gave exit 0; after: 1; invalid pattern, grep status 2, exits 2). shellcheck clean.

Patterns adopted: per-session encrypted sqlite store with a random passphrase kept out of the data dir (keychain instead of the example's plain JSON file), session restore through `restore_session`, dynamic-adapter room list with explicit filter.

No homeserver tests exist yet, so no ignored-test run.

- APIs used: no SDK API. `api/` is SDK-free (std, `thiserror`). `ListDiff` mirrors the 11 `VectorDiff` variants named in the plan (Append, Clear, PushFront, PushBack, PopFront, PopBack, Insert, Set, Remove, Truncate, Reset); the SDK mapping is Task 6/7 and will be confirmed there.
- Design: `ListDiff::apply` ignores out-of-range indices instead of panicking. `TimelineItemKind` has a manual redacted `Debug` (body shown only as byte length), so any event holding a timeline item is safe to log. `SendStatus` is `Sending | Sent | Failed`.
- Negative self-check of `scripts/check-core-api.sh`: with a temporary `use matrix_sdk::Client;` as the first line of `api/mod.rs` it exits 1 (reports the line); a temporary `pub use matrix_sdk::Client;` in `lib.rs` also exits 1; reverted, it exits 0. A missing `api/` dir exits 2 (fail closed).
- CI: `lint` job runs `scripts/check-core-api.sh` after `check-app-deps.sh`.
- Review fix: `Command::SendMessage.text` is now `MessageText` and `CoreEvent::OAuthUrl` holds `AuthUrl`; both have redacted `Debug` (text shows byte length, URL shows scheme+host only) with `expose()` accessors. Tests added for both, plus `ListDiff::Insert` at `index == len`.
- Open issues: none. New human items: none (the `api` approval is already on the checkpoint list).

## Phase 1 / Task 3 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (34 core unit tests, 1 ignored), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`. No homeserver tests exist yet, so no ignored-test run. The real-keychain test was NOT run (Decision 7); only `cargo test -p tessera-core -- --ignored --list` was used, which shows exactly one ignored test, `secrets::tests::keyring_real_keychain_round_trip`, and `--list` shows exactly one test name containing `keyring`.
- TDD: tests were first run against `todo!()` stubs (4 secrets tests and 7 session tests failed), then passed after implementation.
- APIs used: `keyring::Entry::{new (keyring-4.2.0/src/v1.rs:47), set_secret (:81), get_secret (:95), delete_credential (:102)}`, `keyring::Error` re-exported from keyring-core (v1.rs:~38; `Error::NoEntry` at keyring-core-1.0.0/src/error.rs:40). `matrix_sdk::authentication::matrix::MatrixSession` (matrix-sdk-0.19.1/src/authentication/matrix/mod.rs:844, Debug at :855), `authentication::oauth::UserSession` (oauth/mod.rs:1773), `matrix_sdk::SessionTokens` (lib.rs:71; authentication/mod.rs:35, Debug hides tokens), `matrix_sdk::SessionMeta` (lib.rs:28). `rand` 0.10.3: `rand::fill` (rand-0.10.3/src/lib.rs:314), which uses the thread-local ChaCha CSPRNG seeded from the OS (`thread_rng` is a default feature).
- Design:
  - `secrets.rs`: `SecretStore`, `InMemorySecretStore`, `KeyringSecretStore::new(service)`, constants `KEYCHAIN_SERVICE` and `TEST_KEYCHAIN_SERVICE`, `SecretError`. The trait is blocking; Task 4 must call it through `spawn_blocking` (not yet wired, no engine exists).
  - `session.rs` (private module, with a module-level `#[allow(dead_code)]` until Task 4 consumes it): `SessionRecord` and `SessionAuth` have fully manual redacted `Debug` (no tokens, passphrase, or OAuth client id). `to_bytes` returns `CoreError::Storage` over 2560 bytes. Serde error text is never forwarded, because serde can quote values. `from_bytes` rejects versions other than 1. Passphrase: 32 bytes, lowercase hex (64 chars), no new dependency (no base64 crate in core).
  - `SessionAuth::OAuth.client_id` is a `String` (the SDK's `oauth2::ClientId` is converted in Task 9).
- Open issues: none.
- New human item: the real-keychain test (already on the checkpoint list), now covering Linux Secret Service, macOS, and Windows.

## Phase 1 / Task 4 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (40 core unit tests; 4 login tests reported as ignored), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check` (zeroize licence ok), shellcheck (docker) on check-core-api.sh. Ignored-test run: `docker compose up -d --wait && scripts/create-test-users.sh && cargo test -p tessera-core --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring` passed 3 times in a row (4/4), then `docker compose stop`. The real-keychain test was never run (Decision 7). `grep -c` of the ignore string in tests/login.rs = 4 = number of `#[tokio::test]`.
- TDD: login tests were first run before `Core` existed (compile error, unresolved imports). Session-record validation and path tests were added with their code in one step (not run red separately for paths).
- Integration failures found and fixed (not flakes): the first runs timed out because Synapse's default login rate limits (per-address burst 5, failed-attempt limits) returned 429 with a retry_after of minutes. Fix: `dev/synapse/start.sh` now relaxes `rc_login` (dev only); it applies only to a fresh volume, so run `docker compose down -v` once locally. The engine also bounds password login with a 20 s timeout (`LOGIN_TIMEOUT`), because the SDK waits out `retry_after`.
- SDK APIs used (matrix-sdk 0.19.1, registry src/): `ClientBuilder::{server_name_or_homeserver_url client/builder/mod.rs:283, homeserver_url :221, sqlite_store :301, handle_refresh_tokens :534, build :656}`; `ClientBuildError` (:945); `MatrixAuth::login_username` (authentication/matrix/mod.rs:175), `LoginBuilder::{initial_device_display_name login_builder.rs:135, send :174}` (send uses `RequestConfig::short_retry()`); `Client::{restore_session client/mod.rs:1591, session :889, logout :1652, subscribe_to_session_changes :3542, user_id :818, device_id :823, homeserver :711}`; `SessionChange::{UnknownToken, TokensRefreshed}` (client/mod.rs:167); `AuthSession` and `From<MatrixSession>` (authentication/mod.rs:193, :235); `matrix_sdk::Error::{Http, client_api_error_kind}` (error.rs:250, :428) and `HttpError::Reqwest` (:66); `ErrorKind::{Forbidden, UserDeactivated, LimitExceeded}` (ruma-common-0.20.0/src/api/error/kind.rs:124, :357, :168); `Client::devices` (client/mod.rs:2972, test helper only). zeroize 1.9.0: `Zeroizing` (src/lib.rs:604; `serde` feature for Serialize/Deserialize :706).
- Design: `engine.rs` (private; only `Core`, `CoreConfig`, `CoreHandle` are re-exported from lib.rs), `client.rs` (builder + error mapping to fixed messages). Server choice: `homeserver_override`, else the command's `server`, else the MXID's server name; a bare localpart with neither gives a `Login` error. Discovery note: with `@alice:localhost` and no override, discovery tries `https://localhost` and fails, which is why tests pass the override (`HOMESERVER_URL`). Restore failures (missing store dir, invalid record, keychain error) emit `Error`, not `NoSession`, so a transient keychain failure does not push the user into a duplicate login. `LoginOAuth`, `SelectRoom`, `SendMessage` are stubs until Tasks 7 and 9. OAuth records are refused on restore until Task 9. On `UnknownToken` (hard or soft logout) the record, marker and store dir are deleted and `SessionExpired` is emitted.
- Review carry-overs: (a) `session_store_dir` is an allowlist (1..=128 of `[A-Za-z0-9_-]`, Windows reserved names rejected; tests include "C:x", "C:", "a:b", "CON", "x."). (b) data root, `sessions/` and the session dir are created 0o700, `last_account` is 0o600 and tightened if older (Unix test). (c) `SessionRecord::from_bytes` checks session_id, homeserver URL (https, or http only for localhost/127.0.0.0/8/::1) and user_id == auth user id, with non-secret `CoreError::Storage`. (d) every `SecretStore` and file-system call goes through `spawn_blocking`. (e) the module-wide `allow(dead_code)` in session.rs is gone. (f) SDK errors map to constant messages; no SDK error text is forwarded or logged. (g) `.expose()` is used only at the login call; nothing logs exposed values.
- (h) `scripts/check-core-api.sh` new rules, each with a negative self-check (temporary edit, exit 1, reverted, exit 0): multi-line `pub use matrix_sdk::{ Client, };` in lib.rs fails; `pub mod engine;` in lib.rs fails; `pub(crate) mod foo;` in lib.rs fails; `pub use crate::engine::Core as X;` in api/mod.rs fails; the same split across lines (`pub use crate::` newline `session::..`) fails; `pub use crate::api::CoreError as Y;` in api/ is allowed (exit 0). Allow-list: api, paths, secrets. shellcheck clean.
- Zeroize (item i): `Password` and `MessageText` hold `Zeroizing<String>` (clones zeroize separately); `SessionRecord.store_passphrase` is `Zeroizing<String>`; `SessionRecord::to_bytes` returns `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>`; the passphrase generator zeroizes its 32 raw bytes and the hex string wipes on drop; `InMemorySecretStore` stores `Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>`; `SecretStore::get` returns `Option<Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>>` (both backends). Known limitation: the SDK-owned `MatrixSession`/`UserSession` tokens (and the SDK's own copies inside `Client`) are not zeroized by us, and `AuthUrl` is not wiped; no SDK type is wrapped or forked. Residual copies can also exist in serde_json intermediate buffers and the keyring backend.
- Open issues: none. Parallel integration tests share the three accounts: bob is used only by the restore test (it counts devices).
- New human items: none beyond the existing list (real keychain restore on each OS is still covered by the keyring item); `docker compose down -v` once to pick up the relaxed rc_login.
- Review fix (Security Medium): password login accepted plain `http://` homeservers (caller `server`/`homeserver_override`, or an https `.well-known` returning an http `base_url`) while restore rejected them. `build_client(ServerSpec::NameOrUrl)` now applies the restore policy (`session::is_allowed_homeserver_url`: https, or http only for localhost/loopback) to an explicit http(s) URL before the SDK contacts it, and again to the resolved `client.homeserver()` before any credential is sent. Failure emits `LoginFailed(Login("the homeserver must use https"))` and the half-created store dir is removed as for other failures. Any future OAuth login must also use `build_client`. Tests: `homeserver_policy_on_resolved_urls` (unit) and `http_non_loopback_homeserver_is_refused_before_login` (no server needed, so not `#[ignore]`; asserts the exact message, empty secret store, no store dir, no marker); seen failing first. Local-homeserver tests (http://127.0.0.1:8008) still pass.
- Review fix (Security Medium, follow-up): `build_client` now calls `.respect_login_well_known(false)`. Verified in matrix-sdk 0.19.1 registry source: setter `client/builder/mod.rs:383` (default `true` at :165, passed to the client at :756); consumed in `Client::maybe_update_login_well_known` at `client/mod.rs:1576`, which otherwise calls `set_homeserver` without a scheme check. Defence in depth: `client::ensure_homeserver_after_login` re-checks `client.homeserver()` right after a successful login and before `persist_new`; on failure there is no server logout (no request over http), the client is dropped, the caller removes the store dir, and `LoginFailed(Login("the homeserver must use https"))` is emitted. Tests: `recheck_rejects_cleartext_non_loopback_homeserver`, `recheck_accepts_https_and_loopback_http` (no network; seen failing first as a compile error). No mock login-with-well_known test: the setter was verified in source instead.
- Known limitations: (1) `LOGIN_TIMEOUT` can orphan a server-side device if the server accepted the login before the timeout. (2) The SDK/reqwest default redirect policy could resend the `/login` body on a 307/308 redirect to http (upstream behaviour; only reachable from the already trusted https endpoint).

Item `id` is the SDK's `unique_id`, stable across local echo to remote echo. ### Task 8 follow-up (review Critical, Security Medium x2, Low)
- Supervisor, final behaviour (`sync.rs`, SDK facts checked in matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `src/sync_service.rs`: `SyncServiceInner::start` sets `Running` at once, before any request succeeds (:503-510); with offline mode a sync error sets `Offline`, polls `/versions`, then sets `Running` and restarts at once (:313-328, `offline_check` :158-220); `stop()` from Running/Offline ends in `Idle` (:657)).
  - Stability window: `STABLE_RESET` = 30 s. The attempt count and the offline-cycle count reset only when a `Running` has lasted 30 s without a failure (evaluated when the next failure arrives, with an injectable `SyncControl::now`). Before this, `Running` reset the count at once, so a persistent failure stayed at ~1 s forever. Now `Error`/`Terminated` give attempts 1, 2, 3 ... with 1, 2, 4 ... s sleeps (cap 60 s, jitter as before).
  - Offline-cycle throttle (human decision: throttle in the supervisor, offline mode kept, `SyncState(Offline)` still reported): a `Running` that is not stable before the next `Offline` is an unstable cycle. After 3 in a row the supervisor emits `Reconnecting { attempt }`, calls `stop()`, waits `backoff_delay(attempt)`, then `start()`; the attempt counter is shared with the `Error` path; the next unstable cycle after a throttle throttles again with a longer delay; a stable `Running` resets everything. A genuine drop that recovers to a stable `Running` is never throttled. The `Idle` caused by our own `stop()` is swallowed so it does not show as flapping.
  - Remaining visible sequence under a persistent failure: `Running`, `Reconnecting{1}`, `Running`, `Reconnecting{2}` ... (the `Running` is the SDK's own, set by `start()`); the attempt number now grows instead of repeating `Reconnecting{1}`.
  - Tests (fake with a virtual clock; `restart` pushes `Running` and `stop` pushes `Idle` into the state stream like the real SDK): Error/restart/Error/restart/Error gives sleeps 1, 2, 4 s and attempts 1, 2, 3; a stable `Running` resets; a `Running` 1 s short of the window does not; rapid Offline cycles throttled with sleeps 1, 2, 4 s and 3 stops; a single Offline then stable `Running` is not throttled; stable `Running` after a throttle resets. `backoff_delay` tests unchanged.
  - Red run (before the supervisor change, new tests against the old logic): `cargo test -p tessera-core --lib sync::` gave `6 passed; 6 failed`: a_running_that_stays_stable_resets_the_attempt_count, a_stable_running_after_a_throttle_resets_everything, backoff_is_capped_during_a_long_outage, rapid_offline_cycles_are_throttled_with_growing_delays, a_running_just_short_of_the_window_does_not_reset, error_reconnects_with_growing_backoff_when_running_is_unstable. Green after: 12 passed.
- `scripts/check-core-api.sh` now also scans the other allow-listed public modules (`paths`, `secrets`, `logging`: `<name>.rs` and a same-named directory) for SDK crate names, failing closed when a module has neither. The comment now says logging exposes `DEFAULT_FILTER`, `LogInitError` and `init`. shellcheck clean (run with the `koalaman/shellcheck:stable` docker image, `--network none`; not installed locally). Negative self-checks: `// matrix_sdk::Client` appended to logging.rs exits 1 and names the file; `// ruma` appended to paths.rs exits 1; secrets.rs moved away exits 2 ("public module secrets not found"); all reverted, script exits 0.
- Checks: full gate and the ignored run (2 runs in a row, 9/9 each, then `docker compose stop`) pass; keyring test skipped.
- Open item for Task 10: the CLI help and README must warn that `RUST_LOG=debug` or `trace` makes the SDK log request URIs and room/user identifiers. Also note in the CLI help that the SDK's own `error!`-level lines (matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `src/sync_service.rs` ~:425, `{:#?}` of the sync error, which can include request URLs) are visible at the default filter (`warn,tessera_core=info`), so stderr should not be shared or posted unreviewed.

### Task 8 follow-up 2 (review Security Medium and Low)
- Coalescing (verified in eyeball 0.8.8 `src/subscriber.rs:123-131`: `poll_next_ref` yields only the latest value once the version moved; `Subscriber::next_now` marks the current value observed). SDK transitions (matrix-sdk-ui 0.19.1 `sync_service.rs`): `Offline` is set only inside the supervisor loop after a failure while `Running` (:315); `Running` is set by `SyncServiceInner::start` (:509) or after `Offline` (:328); `SyncService::start` while `Running` does nothing (:635). So a repeated state proves a missed transition. Final rules in `supervise`: an `Offline` with no `Running` seen since the last `Offline`/restart counts as an unstable cycle; a `Running` right after a `Running` counts as an unstable cycle (missed `Offline`) and restarts the stability window; the `Running` produced by our own `restart` is expected (window starts when `restart` returns). The initial state is read with `next_now()` (not `get()`), so it is not delivered twice. Remaining blind spot: a coalesced own-restart `Running` that hides a missed cycle is read as the expected echo (only possible during the `restart` call itself).
- No consumer stall: `supervise` publishes through a `StateSink`; production uses a `tokio::sync::watch` latest-value slot (`WatchSink`) drained by a forwarder task (`forward_latest`) that waits on the 256-slot event channel. Supervision never waits for the consumer, so blocked time cannot look like a stable `Running`. Intermediate `SyncState`s may be dropped; the latest is always delivered. Public API unchanged.
- Uncancellable SDK stop/restart: the supervisor is no longer aborted. `Syncing` holds a `CancelTrigger`; `Active::stop_tasks` cancels it and awaits the supervisor task, which checks cancellation between steps (waiting for a state, the backoff sleep, before `restart`) but always runs an in-flight `SyncService::stop`/`start` to the end. Only then are the room list forwarder aborted and `service.stop()` called, so logout and UnknownToken delete the store only after the SDK stop fully completed.
- Tests (first, against plumbing with the old logic): red run `cargo test -p tessera-core --lib sync::` gave `13 passed; 5 failed`: coalesced_running_between_offlines_still_counts_a_cycle, coalesced_offline_between_runnings_counts_a_cycle, a_missed_offline_does_not_make_the_window_look_stable, cancelling_during_stop_still_finishes_the_stop, a_stalled_consumer_does_not_stop_the_backoff (the last one failed on a flawed final assertion that I then removed; against the old blocking `events.send` it is red by construction, not recorded). Green after: 18 passed. New fake modes: `Fake::coalescing` (no echo of `Running`/`Idle`) with a `coalesce` helper that keeps only the last of several states; cancel fired from inside `stop`.
- Checks (all pass): fmt, clippy (-D warnings), `cargo test --workspace --locked` (83 core unit tests; 9 homeserver tests ignored), check-app-deps, check-core-api, cargo deny; ignored run 9/9 (4 login + 2 room_list + 3 timeline), then `docker compose stop`. Keyring test skipped.

### Follow-up fixes (human-approved)
- Security Low/Medium (Task 4/7): a login response's user ID is checked before anything is persisted, on every path (bare localpart, full MXID, explicit URL, homeserver override). Rule: (1) `client::login_account_matches`: a full MXID needs exact equality, a bare localpart an equal localpart (exact, no case folding); (2) `client::server_name_bound` binds the returned user ID's server name to the homeserver actually used (`client.homeserver()`, after `ensure_homeserver_after_login`): accept if the client was built by discovery from a bare server name equal to the returned one; or, narrow local-test exception, the homeserver is a loopback http URL and the returned name is exactly `localhost` (`.env.test` `HOMESERVER_DOMAIN`), without discovery; otherwise the returned server name is resolved by `.well-known` discovery (`client::discover_homeserver_url`) and the discovered URL must have the same origin (scheme, host, port with default ports) as `client.homeserver()`; discovery failure or mismatch refuses. Discovery API (matrix-sdk 0.19.1, `src/client/builder/`): `ClientBuilder::server_name` (`mod.rs:235`) on a throwaway client with the default in-memory store, which runs `HomeserverConfig::discover` (`homeserver_config.rs:63`, `ServerName` arm `:76-87`, lookup in `discover_homeserver` `:193`) with no fallback to a URL; the result must also pass the https/loopback policy, with a 10 s timeout. (3) Defence in depth in `persist_new` (`Engine::existing_record_session`): an existing secret-store record for the returned user ID with a different homeserver origin is refused and left untouched; one for the same origin is replaced and its old store dir (validated session_id) is removed after the new record is saved. Refusal (either place): no secret-store write, no `last_account` write, no logout request, store dir removed, `LoginFailed("the homeserver returned a different account")`. Unit tests use injected discovery results (no network): the two gaps, the delegated case (`@u:example.org` served by `https://matrix-client.example.org`), same-origin comparison, the loopback exception, and the existing-record check with `InMemorySecretStore`. No integration test for a lying homeserver (none available locally); the existing local-homeserver tests cover the `localhost` exception.
- Security Low (Task 6): `tests/login.rs::files_under` tolerates only `ErrorKind::NotFound` and panics on any other `read_dir` or dir-entry error.
- Security Low (Task 5): the CI `integration` job has `timeout-minutes: 20`; actionlint clean.
- Checks: full gate and the ignored run (see commit) pass.

## Phase 1 / Task 9 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace --locked` (89 core unit tests; 10 homeserver tests reported as ignored), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`, `cargo deny check`; ignored run `cargo test -p tessera-core --locked -- --ignored --skip keyring` 10/10 (incl. the new `tests/oauth.rs`), then `docker compose stop`.
- TDD: the unit tests (client metadata JSON, auth-URL policy, error mapping, 127.0.0.1 listener, OAuth record restore path) were seen failing to compile before the code existed; the integration test was seen failing (`Internal("OAuth login is not implemented yet")` instead of `OAuthNotSupported`) before the engine change.
- Design: `crates/core/src/oauth.rs` (metadata, `check_auth_url`, `map_oauth_error`, `spawn_redirect_listener`, `authorize`). `LoginOAuth` runs in a spawned task (build client, `authorize`) so that `CancelOAuth` and `Shutdown` stay responsive; the engine loop awaits the task (`next_oauth`) and `on_oauth_done` then runs the same `adopt_login` as a password login (https/loopback recheck, returned account bound to the server asked for, no cross-origin record overwrite, persist, sync). `persist_new`, restore, and `rewrite_record` now handle both session kinds through `SessionAuth::{to_auth_session, from_auth_session}`, so refreshed OAuth tokens are written back to the keychain. `CancelOAuth` aborts the pending authorization (`abort_login`), the task ends with `Cancelled`, `LoginFailed(Cancelled)` is emitted, and the store directory is removed. Unrequested additions: a 10 minute limit on the browser step, and refusing a second login while one is pending.
- Security carry-overs applied: the redirect listener binds `127.0.0.1` only (`LocalServerIpAddress::Localhostv4`); the auth URL must be `https` (or loopback `http`) without userinfo before `OAuthUrl` is emitted; SDK OAuth error text is never forwarded (fixed messages).
- APIs used (matrix-sdk-0.19.1, `src/`): `authentication/oauth/mod.rs` `OAuth::login` :923, `finish_login` :958, `abort_login` :1095, `full_session` :596, `restore_session` (via `Client::restore_session`, `AuthSession::OAuth(Box<OAuthSession>)` `authentication/mod.rs:198`), `Client::session`/`logout` `client/mod.rs:889`/`:1652` (both dispatch on OAuth); `auth_code_builder.rs` `OAuthAuthCodeUrlBuilder::build`; `registration.rs` `ClientMetadata::new`, `Localized::new`, `OAuthGrantType::AuthorizationCode`, `ApplicationType::Native`; `error.rs` `OAuthError`, `OAuthDiscoveryError::NotSupported`, `OAuthClientRegistrationError::NotSupported`; `utils/local_server.rs` `LocalServerBuilder::{ip_address, spawn}` :91/:135, `LocalServerIpAddress::Localhostv4` :303; `utils/mod.rs:276` `From<QueryString> for UrlOrQuery`. Registry path: `~/.cargo/registry/src/*/matrix-sdk-0.19.1/`. I did not read the repo's `examples/oauth_cli` (not in the tarball).
- Open issues: the SDK validates the authorization server's metadata URLs itself (`validate_urls`), but Tessera does not re-check them beyond the auth URL. A cancel during client build or registration drops that future (the network call is abandoned, not awaited); `finish_login` is deliberately not raced against cancel, so a cancel in that window waits for it (bounded by the 5 s shutdown timeout) and a login that still succeeds is logged out and discarded. A stray local GET to the redirect port makes that login fail (state check), by design of the SDK listener (one request). Local MAS does not exist, so the real flow and token refresh are unverified.
- New human items (also in the Phase 1 checklist): OIDC login on matrix.org through the CLI; the session survives a CLI restart; CLI kept running over 10 minutes so MAS refreshes the access token, then restart and confirm restore works.

## Phase 1 / Task 10 notes
- Checks run (all pass): `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets --all-features -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace` (bin unit tests for args, REPL parsing, room resolution, status lines, terminal-control sanitising, help text), `scripts/check-app-deps.sh`, `scripts/check-core-api.sh`; ignored run `cargo test --workspace -- --ignored --skip keyring` against the local Synapse (includes `tests/cli.rs::ephemeral_password_login_and_quit`), then `docker compose stop`.
- TDD: `tests/cli.rs` was seen failing to compile (no `CARGO_BIN_EXE_tessera-cli`) and the bin unit tests were seen failing against `todo!()` stubs before the implementation.
- Design: `crates/core/src/bin/tessera-cli.rs` uses only `Core::start`, `Command`, `CoreEvent` and API types (a test greps the source for `matrix_sdk`, `ruma`, `eyeball`, `imbl`). One stdin reader task feeds lines to the loop; stdin is not read while a password login is in flight, so `/rooms` queued behind a login runs after it. `--password-stdin` reads the password from the next stdin line, otherwise `rpassword::prompt_password`. `--ephemeral` uses `InMemorySecretStore` and `<tmp>/tessera-cli-<128-bit random hex>`, removed at exit; it cannot be combined with `--data-dir`. Room names, senders, and bodies are printed with control characters replaced by `?` (terminal escape injection). Room indices are 0-based. Own sent messages are echoed back through the timeline as `[room] sender: body`.
- APIs used: only tessera-core's own (`Core::start` engine.rs:72, `CoreConfig`, `CoreHandle`, `Command`, `CoreEvent`, `ListDiff::apply`), `rpassword` 7.5.4 `prompt_password` (`~/.cargo/registry/src/*/rpassword-7.5.4/src/lib.rs:270`), `rand::fill` (already used in session.rs), `tokio::io::{stdin, AsyncBufReadExt::lines}`. No matrix-sdk or iced API was added.
- Deferred Task 8 reconnection check, run with this CLI (alice, `--ephemeral`, local Synapse; times local):
  - 11:42:05 `Logged in as @alice:localhost`, `[sync] idle`, `[sync] running`
  - 11:42:13 `docker compose stop synapse`; 11:42:15 `[sync] offline` (2 s later; it stayed Offline for the 35 s outage, no Reconnecting)
  - 11:42:49 `docker compose start synapse`; 11:42:53 `[sync] running` (4 s, limit 90 s)
  - then `/select` and `/send` of a unique text: echoed in the timeline, and a REST read of the room's messages as alice found it, so it was delivered. stderr during the outage had the SDK's `ERROR ... matrix_sdk::http_client: Error while sending request` line about every 7.6 s (the SDK's own error lines, as documented below).
  - The unstable-Offline-loop provocation (a server that answers `/versions` while sync fails) was not reproduced: the local Synapse is either fully up or down. That path stays covered by the supervisor unit tests only.
- CLI help warns that `RUST_LOG=debug` or `trace` can make the SDK and HTTP crates log sensitive request data, and that the SDK's own error lines (request URLs) show at the default filter so stderr should not be shared unreviewed. The same help text carries the orphaned-OAuth-session note.
- Open issues:
  - A cancelled or shutdown-aborted OAuth login may leave an orphaned server-side session (device) that the user must revoke from the account settings of their homeserver/MAS.
  - Task 9 follow-up: a shutdown abort during `finish_login` (over 5 s) can also leave such a session, and a failed best-effort logout of a cancelled login leaves it valid server-side.
  - The password prompt blocks the CLI loop while waiting (events queue in the engine's bounded channel, 256); fine for interactive use.
  - `/rooms` prints every room; no paging.
- New human items: see the Phase 1 checklist below (CLI real-account run, restart without prompt, keychain viewer entry `io.github.ga_lep.Tessera` and no token in the data dir).

## Phase 1 / Task 10 review follow-up
- Password prompt race fixed: one reader thread owns the terminal and serves one request at a time (`Request::Line` or `Request::Password`), driven by a `Gate`; a password read is only started when no line read is outstanding, and a password input can never be routed to the REPL (unit tests: `password_is_read_apart_from_repl_lines`, `gate_never_routes_a_password_to_the_repl`, `gate_allows_one_reader_at_a_time`). Real-terminal behaviour is unverified (human list).
- `sanitize` also maps U+200B-U+200F, U+202A-U+202E, U+2066-U+2069, U+FEFF to `?` (unit-tested).
- Ctrl-C: `tokio::signal::ctrl_c` runs alongside the REPL (tokio `signal` feature added to `crates/core/Cargo.toml`, human-approved, no version change) and takes the normal Shutdown and temp-dir cleanup path. With `--ephemeral` the CLI sends `Logout` and waits (10 s) before `Shutdown` so the server session is revoked; documented in the help text. A SIGINT during a login in flight relies on the engine's Shutdown cancel path.
- `--password-stdin` with a terminal on stdin prints a warning to stderr (echo); documented in the help text.

### Task 10 final-review follow-up (human-approved)
- Stuck startup: on `CoreEvent::Error` while Busy and not logged in (restore or login in flight; the engine's `restore()` emits `Error`, not `NoSession`, for an unavailable keychain, an inconsistent or unparsable record, a missing store dir, or a failed `restore_session`) the CLI now prints the error, a fixed hint, and returns to Idle, so stdin is read and `/login` and `/quit` work. Other not-logged-in exits from Busy were checked: `LoginFailed`, `NoSession` (goes to the username prompt), `SessionExpired`, `LoggedOut` already leave Busy. Tests: `error_during_restore_or_login_leaves_busy` (seen failing first: Busy vs Idle), `error_keeps_other_modes`, `other_not_logged_in_failures_leave_busy`, `quit_works_after_a_failed_restore`. While a login or restore is genuinely in flight the CLI still does not read stdin (so piped input queued behind it is not lost); a hung login can only be left with Ctrl-C.
- Terminal echo: Ctrl-C is now a message from a forwarding task to the loop instead of a `select!` arm in `main`. While a hidden password read (`rpassword`) is pending, Ctrl-C is deferred (`ctrl_c_action`, `Cli::interrupt_action`): the CLI prints a note, the read finishes on Enter (rpassword restores echo), the password is dropped, and the CLI exits through the normal shutdown path. With `--password-stdin` no echo is involved, so Ctrl-C quits at once. No new dependency. Tests: `ctrl_c_waits_for_a_hidden_password_read`, `ctrl_c_during_a_password_read_waits_for_the_read_then_quits` (the Ctrl-C tests were seen failing first as compile errors). Real-terminal behaviour is on the human list (item reworded).
- Open issues: a stale record whose store dir is missing still yields `Error` on every restore (core behaviour, not changed here); the user must log in again, which replaces it. Ctrl-C at the password prompt needs Enter to take effect (a second Ctrl-C is deferred too). A tiny window exists between two `ctrl_c()` registrations in the forwarder where a SIGINT could be missed.

### CI triggers follow-up (human-approved)
- `ci.yml` now triggers on `push` to `main`, on `pull_request`, and on `workflow_dispatch`, so a push to a branch with an open PR no longer runs the workflow twice. Feature branches get CI through their PR; a branch without a PR can be run manually via workflow_dispatch; main runs on push after merge. Nothing else in the workflow changed.

- Task 9 follow-up (human-approved): shutdown can no longer hang (the `OAuthUrl` send and the client build are raced against cancel; the engine waits 5 s for the task, then aborts it, waits for the abort so the store is released, and removes the store dir). A cancel that arrives after `finish_login` succeeded now logs the client out (best effort, 5 s) and emits `LoginFailed(Cancelled)` without persisting anything. The account binding was extracted to `client::account_bound` with a test that an OAuth user ID from another server name is refused (OAuth has no typed username, so `server_name_bound` is the only binding).

### CI disk and time follow-up (human-approved)
- `ci.yml`: workflow-level `CARGO_PROFILE_DEV_DEBUG: line-tables-only` (the Ubuntu runner ran out of disk linking ~880 MB debug test binaries). The test job drops the separate `cargo build` step, runs clippy before tests only on non-Linux (the lint job covers Linux), and has `timeout-minutes: 60` (Windows took ~35 min). No disk-freeing step was added; add one (plain shell, no new action) only if Linux still runs out of space.
- rust-cache keys include `CARGO_*` env, so the first run after this change starts with a cold cache; that is expected.
- Not verifiable locally: the effect on the CI runners; check the next CI run.

### Flaky `unread_count_reaches_two` (human-approved)
- Root cause (test race, not a core bug): the room list syncs with `timeline_limit` 1 and `num_unread_messages` is computed client-side from the timeline events the core receives. The test sent both messages right after joining, so if the core's sync only saw the room afterwards (slow CI), it got just the latest event and the count stayed at 1 forever. Reproduced deterministically by sending both messages before the core logs in (count stays 1 for 5 s+), and under `taskset -c 0,1` with CPU hogs (original test failed at `room_list.rs:51`).
- Fix: the test now waits for the room to appear, then sends "first" and waits for unread >= 1, then "second" and waits for unread >= 2. No timeout or sleep change.
- Verified: fixed test 12/12 under `taskset -c 0,1` plus 3 CPU hogs; full `--ignored --skip keyring` suite 6/6 (3 plain, 3 with `taskset -c 0,1 --test-threads 2`); gate and boundary scripts ok; compose stopped.

  - `room_list_service/filters/mod.rs`: re-exports :87-104 (`new_filter_all`, `new_filter_not`, `new_filter_non_left`, `new_filter_category`, `RoomCategory`, `new_filter_space`, `new_filter_normalized_match_room_name`), `BoxedFilterFn` :115; `category.rs:30-46` (`RoomCategory::{People, Group}` from `is_dm()`); `all.rs:19`, `not.rs:21`, `space.rs:23`, `non_left.rs:27`, `normalized_match_room_name.rs:48`.
